1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/nI lAM bar d+ha ra badzra pA NI yak+Sha ma hA ru dra badzra a la dzi hwa b+ha hri dz+hi tan   
1b2(rgyud): tra nA ma/bod skad du/phyag na rdo rje gos sngon po can gnod sbyin drag po chen po rdo rje me lce'i rgyud 
1b3(rgyud): ces bya ba/'phags pa phyag na rdo rje la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus 
1b4(rgyud): gcig na/bcom ldan 'das thams cad 'byung ba'i yum btsun mo'i b+ha ga la bzhugs te/
1b5(rgyud): de nas gnod sbyin drag po bye ba phrag bcu dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 
1b6(rgyud): na drag cing gtum pa'i rigs ji snyed pa thams cad dang thabs gcig go/de nas bcom ldan 'das 
1b7(rgyud): drag po'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas nas g.yas pa nam mkha' la bsgreng/g.yon pa sa la bgrang nas   
1b8(rgyud): spyan bzlog pas phyogs bcur gzigs te/zhal nas hU~M ha zhes pa'i sgra cher byung ngo //de nas  
2a1(rgyud): 'khor thams cad gtso bo mi snang bas yun ring du brgyal bar gyur to//de'i skad cig de nyid la bcom ldan 'das kyi spyi bo'i gtsug nas sangs rgyas thams cad kyis gnang ba/sngags rnam pa 
2a2(rgyud): gsum gyi bdag por gyur pa/phyag brnyan lag na rdo rje zhes bya ba kha dog sngon po cher grags pa/phyag g.yas pa na rdo rje rtse dgu pa thogs pa/g.yon pa na nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po drag po thogs pa zhig 
2a3(rgyud): rgya mtsho chen por zhugs so//de nas nam mkha' lding gi gshog pa bskyod pas rgya mtshor zhugs te/klu'i rgyal po dga' bo dang 'jog pos rang gi bdag po'i spyi bor 'dud do//nye dga' bo dang dung skyong 
2a4(rgyud): gis rna cha byas so//rigs ldan gyis mgul rgyan du byas so//pad ma dang pad ma chen pos lag gdub tu byas so//sprin gyi shugs can gyis ser la khar bcings so//hu lu hu lus ska rags 
2a5(rgyud): su byas so//'brug sgra dang bdud 'joms kyis rkang gdub tu byas te/slar bdag po'i lag pa g.yon par song ngo //de nas gnod sbyin bye ba phrag bcu dang /drag po'i rigs rnams pa rgyal ba las sangs 
2a6(rgyud): te/phyogs kun tu bltas pa las phyag brnyan lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho'i nang na 'dug pa mthong nas/rings par kye ma a la la bdag cag gi gtso bo nyid bzhugs so zhes brjod nas ong pa las phyin te/bltas 
2a7(rgyud): pas cher drag pa skra kham pa gyen du 'phags pa/mig me mdag gi phung po ltar 'bar ba/mche ba gtsigs pa/lce glog ltar 'gyu ba/gsus pa rdzing bu ltar 'khyil ba/rgya mtsho'i zabs su dpag tshad 
2a8(rgyud): brgyad khri yod pa rkang pa'i sbas mor phyin pa/klu'i rgyan cha bdun dang ldan pa/lag pa g.yas na rdo rje rtse dgu pa thogs pa/g.yon na nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po drag po thogs pa/sku byin po la dpag tshad nyis 
2b1(rgyud): 'bum drug khri yod pa bzhugs so//de nas gnod sbyin rnams kyis gsol pa//gtso bo khyod kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba'i don drag po rgyas par gsol cig dang /bdag cag rnams kyis zhu bar ma spobs 
2b2(rgyud): kyis zhes gsol nas/lag na rdo rje la gos sngon po dar la la bai dUr+ya zhu na ma'i nang du btsos pa zhig bla gos su gsol te/stag gi lpags pa drug stong gis sham thabs su bcings so//de nas lag na rdo rje rings 
2b3(rgyud): par bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar song ste/zhabs la spyi bos gtugs te/mdun du snying po dang ldan pa'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas te 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi nam mkha' dang sa gzhi la gzigs 
2b4(rgyud): nas/bar snang du yang spyan gyis 'khor bar gzigs so//de nas kha~M bru~M sa~M zhes bya ba'i sngags brjod do//de nas lag na rdo rje stan las langs nas rdo rje thal mo rang gi snying khar sbyar te/bcom ldan 'das la 
2b5(rgyud): 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ma bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba drag po'i don bdag dang sems can thams cad la phan pa'i phyir zhu na/ci bcom ldan 'das kyis skabs dbye bar gnang ngam zhes gsol ba dang /ci 
2b6(rgyud): yang mi gsung bar bcom ldan 'das gdug pa'i tshogs nges par 'dul ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/zhugs ma thag tu stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na 'khod 
2b7(rgyud): pa'i gdug pa can thams cad snying 'dar bar gyur to//de nas ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs nas lag na rdo rje la gzigs te/byed pa g.yon pa'i chu shing snying po nas drangs te bkug pa'o//byed pa skyes pa'i chu shing 
2b8(rgyud): sgra dang bcas pas brkyang ba byas te gnas so//de nas lag na rdo rje phyag rgya de nyid yang dag par rig ste/tshig 'di skad ces brjod de/khyed la sogs pa'i 'khor dag sa phyogs 'dir ma gnas par phyogs gzhan du dengs 
3a1(rgyud): @#//shig/gal te mi 'gron khyed la sogs pa'i snying 'gas par 'gyur ro zhes brjod pas sgra gnas kyi khung bur sgrib byed kyi tshogs song ste/snga ma bzhin du 'khod do//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me 
3a2(rgyud): lce'i rgyud las gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o////
3b1(rgyud): 'chang rab dga' bas//de nyid rigs su byung ba ste//kun kyang drag po'i las la dga'//snang ba dag par mi mthong bzhin//de nyid la ni nges par brjod//gong du bstan pa gang yin pa//so so dag la gdon mi za//sbyor ba sna 
3b2(rgyud): tshogs nges bsgrubs na//snang bar 'byor pa sna tshogs pa//mngon du rgyas shing nges par gsal//snang ba chen po bdag med par//rgyud pas yongs su 'grub pa yin//kun rdzob gong ma'i tshig lta bur//dngos po 
3b3(rgyud): bsten pa lta bur mthong //don dam 'og ma'i sgra lta bur//nyon mongs kun bsregs rang bzhin gyis//'od gsal ba ru gnas pa yin//snying po don dam la 'jug pa//phyi rim kun rdzob la brten bya//dang por sangs 
3b4(rgyud): rgyas mi gnas shing //snga ma'i bya ba sbyor bar brtsan//dbang thob pa la rlom sems med//bla ma'i man ngag nges pa las//bla ma nyid la ci nus kyis//yang dag par ni mchod par bya//rdo rje ngo bo kun 'byung dang //gdug 
3b5(rgyud): pa thams cad sreg phyir gang //dngos po la 'jug gong ma'i sgra//gong ma'i tshig tu brjod pa yin//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las//nges tshig gi le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
3b6(rgyud): gos sngon lag na rdo rje yis//sangs rgyas mgon la 'di skad gsol//gang zhig bsgrub par bya ba yi//thabs kyi dkyil 'khor gsung du gsol//rig pa'i rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//yang dag don dam dngos gyur 
3b7(rgyud): na//dkyil 'khor lha gzugs med pa ste//'gro ba rtog pa la gnas pas//de yi kun rtog bsal ba'i phyir//lha yi sbyor ba bshad par bya//gang zhig mos par byed pa yis//khru gsum dkyil 
3b8(rgyud): 'khor phyogs bzhi pa//nags sam dur khrod khang stong ngam//gtsug lag khang ngam mchod rten nam//de dag gnas su nges par brtsam//dang por bsten pa rab gnas pas//bu mo bcu drug ston mo byas//bdag gi 
4a1(rgyud): @#//zhag gsum khrus bya zhing //spyi bo lkog ma snying ka ru//g.yang ba gsum gyi yi ge brtag/rams su btsos pa'i gos nyid la//gur gum thig le nges btab ste//de nyid la ni bskon par bya//zas su 
4a2(rgyud): dkar gsum sbyin byas shing //skyes pa dang ni sprad mi bya//snying po 'di nyid 'bum tshad du//rang gis bzlas pas rig pa 'byin//de nas bsnyen pa rdzogs pa las//bu mo nyid la tshig brjod de//yi ge rnam lnga nyid dang yang //
4a3(rgyud): rams dang mthing shun kun 'dus su//rin thang mi brtsi nyor bcug ste//de nyid rigs mthun nang btums la//gnas gang yin par bdag la btang //de yis zhag gsum sbyor ba yis//phyag rgya nges pa nyid dang yang //
4a4(rgyud): snying po gong gi sbyor ba yis//dri chu btab la grangs tshad du//phyin par yang dag bzlas par bya//de nas skud pa sngon po ni//bu mos legs par bkal ba la//sngags kyis dag byas rab mdzes pas//srad bu dgu ru 
4a5(rgyud): nges par sgril//me tog sngon po'i nang btums la//seng ldeng phur ba lnga rnams kyis//srad bu yongs su bri bar bya//rab tu tshad du nges pa yis//phyag rgya ma la ngag brjod pas//de nyid mnyam par bzhag pa yis//
4a6(rgyud): dkyil 'khor legs par byas pa yin//'phang du khru gang tshad byas te//dang por byang shar mtshams nyid nas//kun rten kha dog rigs mthun blang //yongs su bsdus byas nges par bya//gnyis pa gnyis pa nyid du nges//
4a7(rgyud): gsum pa gcig gi tshad dang ldan//de ltar tshad du ldan pa las//shin tu mnyam par 'thas par bya//de nyid rjes su 'jug pa yis//dang po'i srad bu gang yin pas//kha dog ser po'i thig gdab bya//dkyil 'khor 
4a8(rgyud): dgu ru nges pa las//'bru dang sa dang rin chen dang //phye ma nyid du btags pa yis//dkyil du pad ma bcu drug pa//'dab mar ldan par bri bar bya//shar dang lho dang nub byang dang //mtshams rnams su yang de bzhin bri//
4b1(rgyud): le tshe'i pad ma gang yin pa//sna tshogs par ni yang dag bshad//mtshams rnams su ni mchod pa yi//lha mo yongs su dgod par bya//sgo bzhi dag tu khro mo bzhi//sbyor bas yang dag dgod par bya//rin chen pha gu 'dod yon dang //dra 
4b2(rgyud): ba dra phyed yang dag ldan//twa ra na ni bzhis brgyan cing //sna tshogs rdo rje gzhir gnas yin//gdugs dang ri dgas gang yin pa//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par bsgegs//sngags pa byang chub blo ldan pas//keng 
4b3(rgyud): rus phreng ba nyid dang yang //sbrul mgo'i phreng bas rnam par brgyan//rdo rje sna tshogs rab 'bar ba'i//phreng bas phyi rol legs par bskor//zur bzhi dag tu bum pa bzhi//rgyan gyis rdzogs par yang dag bri//de dkyil phyag 
4b4(rgyud): na rdo rje nyid//snga ma'i tshul gyis yongs su gnas//shar du khams gsum rnam rgyal nyid//yang dag par ni bri bya ste//kha dog dmar po nyid la yang //dbu gsum phyag ni drug pa'i dbyibs//zhabs bzhi brkyang bskum tshul 
4b5(rgyud): du bzhugs//pho nyi rdo rje nges pa las//mo nyi dgra sta yang dag ldan//bar ma gnyis kyis 'ching byed pa//gung mo mthe bo nges 'dud nas//gzhan rnams yongs su brkyang ba yin//de nyid snying po gang yin pa//snying kar 
4b6(rgyud): yang dag bzung byas nas//de 'og pho yis zhags pa 'dzin//mo yis ral gri bzung byas pa//dang po nyid la dgod par bya//lho ru nag po chen po nyid//kha dog dud sprin lta bu la//zhal gcig phyag ni gnyis pa'i dbyibs//
4b7(rgyud): pho nyid tsan dan beng chen bsnams//mo ni zhags pa yongs su 'dzin//khro zhal gzigs pa mi bzad pas//phyogs bcu rnams su rab tu gzigs//de nyid mgon po gang yin pa//phyogs gnyis par ni rab tu dgod//
4b8(rgyud): nub phyogs gsum par dgod bya ba//lus ngan sras po stobs po che//kha dog ljang ku nyid la yang //cung zad spyan ni rgyas pa nyid//zhal gcig phyag ni gnyi pa la//spyi bor rdo rje rmog gis brgyan//g.yas 
5a1(rgyud): pa nyid la rdo rje can//g.yon pa ne'u le'i rkyal pa 'dzin//zhabs gnyis drang por rab gnas te//shar phyogs bltas pas yongs su gnas//byang phyogs bzhi par dgod bya ba//bdud rtsi 'khyil ba yongs 
5a2(rgyud): su gnas//kha dog sngon po de nyid la//tsham rngam khro gnyer brjid pa'i zhal//g.yas bskum g.yon brkyang stangs stabs tshul//phyag gnyis brang du rab bsnol te//g.yon du nges par cung zad gzigs//lhor bltas yang 
5a3(rgyud): dag dgod par bya//byang shar rdo rje gnod sbyin te//kha dog dmar nag 'jigs pa la//g.yas brkyang g.yon bskum rab bzhugs nas//phyag g.yas gri gug rnon po 'phyar//g.yon pa cang te'u drag po rdung //gtso bo la 
5a4(rgyud): bltas dgod par bya//shar lho rdo rje ro langs nyid//kha dog sngo skya rab 'bar la//mi bzang 'bar ba 'jigs pa'i gzugs//cung zad g.yas su nges par gzigs//g.yas brkyang g.yon bkum tshul gnas te//
5a5(rgyud): rdo rje lcags sgrog rab bzung nas//gung srin la sogs gsum po rnams//dam por bkug pas yongs su bsdams//'theb 'dzub bzhi rnams rab brkyang ste//spyi bor nges par bsnol bar bsnams//lho nub 
5a6(rgyud): rdo rje mkha' 'gro ste//kha dog dmar skya nyid la yang //shin tu mi bzad 'jigs pa'i gzugs//zhabs gnyis mthil sbyar btsugs pa la//nam mkha' gru bzhi pa ru 'dod//lag gnyis spyi bo thal sbyar nas//
5a7(rgyud): me lce 'bar ba yongs su 'dzin//thod pa lnga yis mgo brgyan la//gtso bo la bltas dgod par bya//nub byang bzhi pa'i mtshams su yang //rdo rje 'jigs byed zhes bya ba//kha dog sngo nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//
5a8(rgyud): zhal gsum phyag ni drug pa la//zhabs gnyis dag tu rnam par bshad//g.yas g.yon dang po glang chen gyi//lpags pa yongs su brkyang ba 'dzin//de 'og g.yas pa dgra sta la//g.yon pa rdo rje yang dag 'dzin//
5b1(rgyud): gsum pa'i g.yas na lcags kyu ste//g.yon na mi mgo brlon pa bsnams//brkyang bskum tshul du gnas nas kyang //gtso bo nyid la kha bltas bzhugs//mtshams kyi gru chad gang yin par//'od skyed ma dang yid 'phrog 
5b2(rgyud): ma//kun bzang ma dang glu sgra can//rang gi phyogs kyi kha dog la//shin tu zhi bas ldem bag can//phyag g.yas su ni me tog dang //dri dang zhal zas sgra brnyan rnams//gar gyis rol par yang dag ldan//num cung zad 
5b3(rgyud): 'bur ba la//dar dag dkar pos smad g.yogs pas//gtso bo la bltas mdzes pa'i gzugs//phyi yi grwa ru dgod par bya//sgo rnams su ni sgo skyong ni//srog gcod ma dang dbugs rngub ma//khrag mthung ma dang sha za 
5b4(rgyud): ma//zhal gcig phyag ni gnyis pa la//stag dang seng ge sbrul dang yang //dom du yang dag gnas pa ste//phyag g.yas thob rkyal pa dang //hom gzar gri gug rnon po bsnams//g.yon pa rnams ni zhags pa nyid//kun 
5b5(rgyud): kyang 'jigs par yongs su 'dzin//rang gi phyogs kyi kha tog ste//kun kyang nag po shin tu bsreg/g.yon brkyang g.yas ni bskums pa nyid//mche ba gtsigs shing rngam pa'i sku//shin tu khro gnyer brjid pa'i zhal//
5b6(rgyud): gar stabs kyis rol bde chen gnas//cung zad g.yon du gzigs pa yis//ha la ha la'i sgra yang sgrogs//gzhan yang lha rnams de dag kun//thod pa lnga yis brgyan byas nas//rgyan cha lnga dang yang dag ldan//kun 
5b7(rgyud): kyang stag gi lpags pa yis//smad ni yongs su dkris pa yin//de stengs gdan ni rab gnas pa//gtso bo nyid la nying zla yis//phrag pa nyid la 'khor bar rgyu//tshangs pa dbang po lha chen gnyis//zhabs kyis yongs 
5b8(rgyud): su mnan pa yin//shar gyi lha chen rnam rgyal nyid//keng rus steng na bzhugs pa bzhin//gzhan yang de ltar gnas pa yin//sa bon gnas pa gang yin pa//sgra dang bcas pa shes par bya//dang po gtso bo skyed byed 
6a1(rgyud): @#//gang //hU~M las nges par rdzogs bya zhing //gzhan rnams 'di las 'byung bar bsam//hU~M ma~M ba~M oM//ya~M b+ha~M Na~M b+ha~M//phra~M ma~M ro~M Sh+ta~M/ba~M sha~M ba~M sta~M//de dag las byung sngags pa yis//'od dang bcas par bde gshegs 
6a2(rgyud): kyis//zhal nas 'byung bar yang dag ldan//de las rigs pa gang zhig gis//'bar 'phro nges par bskyed par bya//dang po kun kyang gcig pa yis//ba~M las gyur pa sna tshogs pa//yang dag par ni bskyed par byed//
6a3(rgyud): de 'og ka~M las rab bskyed pas//skam por yongs su brtag pa yin//de dag rab rdzogs sngags pa yi//sku ru yongs su rdzogs byas la//bdag gis rnal 'byor yang dag 'grub//de nas sngags pa blo ldan pas 
6a4(rgyud): rigs su nges pa gang yin pa//thabs 'di la ni 'jug par bya//kun la 'jug par gyur pa yi//khyab pa'i bdag po chen po ni//rigs kyi gtso bos spyi la khyab//mdun ni rdo rje'i rigs yin te//lha min la sogs 
6a5(rgyud): nges par 'jig/g.yas pa nyid na gang gnas pa//rin chen rigs su nges pa ste//sha za la sogs nges par 'jig/rgyab na gnas pa gang yin pa//pad ma'i rigs su yang dag ldan//nor la sogs pa gang dgod 
6a6(rgyud): pa//re ba bzhin du skong bar byed//g.yon na nges par gnas pa gang //las kyi rigs su nges pa las//klu dang gnod sbyin la sogs pa//ma rungs pa rnams yongs su 'jig/gzhan yang lhag ma thams cad kun//
6a7(rgyud): pho nya'i tshul du nges par gnas//dang po nyi ma shar ba na//las kyi sbyor ba sna tshogs pa//so so dag la snying po nyid//gong du pu~M las rab rdzogs pa'i//brgya rtsa brgyad dag nges par bzlas//hU~M gi sgra dang rab 
6a8(rgyud): ldan pas//bdag nyid kha nas me 'bar gsal//phyag rgya snang bar bcas byas te//de nyid stangs stabs bskyod pa yis//gdug pa thams cad sreg byed dang //bde gshegs mngon par dgyes par bya//rdo 
6b1(rgyud): rje dril bu gzung byas nas//khro bo'i cha lugs gang yin pas//phyogs rnams rmongs par byed pa yin//lha mo'i gzugs 'chang gang yin pas//gzhan gyi sems kyang 'phrog par byed//re mig rnams kyi bar gang 
6b2(rgyud): yin//me lce 'bar ba yongs su bri//'khor lo'i 'og tu gnas bya ba//chos 'byung phyag rgya nges par bri//thabs dang shes rab ldan pa la//kun 'byung yum du nges par gsal//rten 'brel yang dag 'byung ba yis//kun 
6b3(rgyud): rdzob la ni don dam brten//don dam nyid la kun rdzob kyang //brten pa nyid du yang dag gnas//de bas snang ba mi gsal bar//chos 'byung phyag rgya ngesapar yang //rgyas pa la sogs ma yin gyi//drag po'i dkyil 'khor 
6b4(rgyud): yin pa dang //de bzhin dbyibs su rang gsal ba//dran dang bcas pas bri bar bya//mthu dang ldan pa'i gang zag la//stobs ni shin tu nges par skye//dkyil 'khor lha gnyis mngon bsgrubs pas//lus la gzi brjid 'jug 
6b5(rgyud): pa'o//rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las dkyil 'khor dang lha'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////lag na rdo rje gos sngon gyis//'jig rten mgon la 'di skad gsol//dkyil 'khor lha rnams dgyes bya ba//thabs 
6b6(rgyud): de gang lags gsung du gsol//nang gi bdag nyid can dang yang //phyi yi khrus sogs rab byas la//rab gsal la sogs rgyun bcad nas//gang yang rung ba'i lo nyid la//ston zlar gnas brtsams nas//lha min bla dang me tog 
6b7(rgyud): gi/drag po dang po de nyid la//bdag gis sbyor ba nges par brtsam//de nas dang po bdag nyid lus nyid la//rgyan cha lnga yis rab brgyan te//lus la gos dag sngon po nyid//bla gos smad g.yogs yang dag bcings//spyi bo la sogs 
6b8(rgyud): gnas lnga ru//sbrul gyis yongs su bcings par bya//rang nyid dkyil 'khor shar sgo ru//'dug pas sbyor ba brtsam par bya//de nas dang po bla re gang //steng du nges par phub byas la//zur bzhi dag du ba dan bzhi//sngon pos 
7a1(rgyud): @#//yongs su spyad par bya//bla re dag gi dkyil dag nas//skud pa la brgyus me tog gi/phreng ba yongs su spyad par bya//de nas snga ma'i tshul gnas la//gong du bstan pa'i gnas rnams su//bdag gi las 'di 
7a2(rgyud): nges par brtsam//gtor ma'i las rnams kun rdzogs dang //mchod pa rnam pa lnga rnams dang //seng ldeng phur pa sor brgyad la//sngags 'di yis ni yang dag bsngag/oM badzra kI la ya sarba bag+h+nin ha na ha na hU~M phaT/oM 
7a3(rgyud): badzra bany+tsa dz+nyA na sho d+ha ya sarba tsa k+Shu kA ra ya hU~M//sngags de dag gis phur bu dang //skud pa lnga la lan grangs ni//nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas byas la//tsan dan la sogs dri lnga yis//gos par yang dag bcang par bya//
7a4(rgyud): de nas sngags pa blo ldan pas//bdag nyid bsrung ba'i 'khor lo brtsam//rang gi phyi rol mtshams su ni//mi 'gyur las ni bsil ldan bsgom//de steng sdud pa'i dngos po gang //kha dog nag po yang dag bsam//de 
7a5(rgyud): las khyab byed rigs lnga pa//yang dag par ni bsam par bya//de nas yongs su rdzogs pa las//bdud rtsi 'khyil par bdag bsam ste//rdo rje sdigs mdzub phyag rgya yis//sngags bzlas pas ni bgegs bskrad la//
7a6(rgyud): rigs gtso'i rtse mo de nyid la//ka~M las lus skyob bsam bya ste//spyi bor gnas pas khyab par bsam//de nas gtor ma de nyid la//bdug pa la sogs gang yin dang //skye ba bzhis bsdus sems can la//las kyi bar 
7a7(rgyud): chad mi 'gyur phyir//bsam pa rdzogs par rin thang bya//de nas bdag nyid dad pa yis//mchod pa rnam pa lnga rnams ni//bsam pa sngags kyis dbul bar bya//gong ma'i stangs stabs las bya ba//g.yas pa nges par 
7a8(rgyud): bskums pa las//g.yon pa legs par brkyang byas te//g.yas pa'i lag pas rdo rje nyid//phyar nas g.yon gyi be con dang //rtse sprad spyi bor gnas byas la//g.yon pa'i be con g.yas spos la//g.yas pa rdo rje g.yon 
7b1(rgyud): pa yi//lag pas gzung nas snying khar gnan//be con nges par bskor ba yis//phyogs bzhi mtshams su nges par gdab//kha na hU~M gi sgra 'bar zhing //sna tshogs rdo rje gar thabs kyis//lan gsum bar du bsor bar 
7b2(rgyud): bya//de nas gdug pa gang yin pa//phyogs bzhi dag du song ba las//sa phyogs nges par btsang bar 'gyur//de nas thig sogs gdab pa yis//sta gon legs par gnas pa dang //rim pa bzhin du dbang bskur la//
7b3(rgyud): rmi ltas nges par gdab par bya//bzang ngan bltas dag dbab pa dang //bzlog dang mi bzlog bya ba gang //shes rab can gyis gsal bar brtag/sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor bzhengs bya'i phyir//bdag gis drag po dang po 
7b4(rgyud): la//phye ma lnga la sngags dag dang //phyag rgya bcas pas byin gyis brlab//de nas snod dag thod pa la//byas pas rdo rje phyag rgyas gdab//nges par las kyi sbyor ba tsam//phyogs su nges par bskor ba yin//dbugs rgyu ba yi 
7b5(rgyud): dngos po gang //yi ge mngon par gsal ba yis//rgyud 'di 'chad par bri bar bya//skabs kyi skad cig shes pa yis//rab tu khro gnyer bstan par bya//dang po nag po bri bya zhing //gnyis pa dkar por nges par dgye//gsum pa 
7b6(rgyud): nyid ni ser po ste//bzhi pa dmar po yang dag ldan//lnga pa ljang khu bri bya zhing //drug pa kha dog sna tshogs rgyas//dkyil 'khor las rnams rdzogs pa dang //rdzas su yongs su ldan byas te//grub bzhi dag tu byung shar gyi//
7b7(rgyud): mtshams nas sa kang bu mo yis//blangs pa las byas mchod rten ni//sngon po bzhi dag gzhag par bya//de yi bskor ba'i rtse mo la//grong gi srad bu btags byas te//me tog sngon po'i phreng ba yis//'khor bar yongs 
7b8(rgyud): su brgyan par bya//bum pa sngon po bcu bdun nyid//mtha' ma'i phyogs su nges par bzhag/sman dang spos dang snying po dang //lan tshwa rtsi thog la sogs pas//legs par bkang bas gnas pa yin//rgyan rnams kun 
8a1(rgyud): @#//gyis rab brgyan nas//lus la mngon par phyogs par bzhag/de nas sngags pa blo ldan pa//grangs dang nges par rab sbyar la//zhal zas zur med legs pa dang //dri dang snang ba gang yin dang //byug pa'i dri 
8a2(rgyud): dang tshangs skud dang //phreng bar la gri be con rnams//yang dag pa ru dgod par bya//gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan dang //phyogs su nges par yang dag gnas//de nas bdag po gang zhig gis//na bza' dar yug sngon po la//
8a3(rgyud): gur gum thig les legs bris la//de nyid la ni dbul bar bya//yol ba sngon pos phyogs bskor la//phyi ma rnams kyis snang mi bya//gal te snang bar gyur pa na//rang gi lus las ma gtogs pa'i//gzhan rnams brlag 
8a4(rgyud): par 'gyur ba yin//de bas legs par bsgrims pa yis//phyi rol gzhan la bstan mi bya//gang zag gcig pur gnas pa yis//las kyi sbyor ba 'di brtsam bya//phyag dag sngon du song ba yis//mchod pa'i las la mngon 
8a5(rgyud): par bzhag/bdag nyid las kyi phyag rgyar gnas//bsam pa mngon du gyur pa yis//kun gyi 'bras bu gang yin pa//bdag gis gsal bar rab tu brtag/sngags dang phyag rgya rab ldan pa'i//mchod pa 'di dag nges pa 
8a6(rgyud): las//bgegs bsal ye shes rab dbab pa'i//rnal 'byor rdzogs par yang dag gnas//rang rang rigs kyi phyag rgya yis//sngags bzlas pa yis dbul bar bya//oM na mo nI lA~M ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni maN Da la de ba tA 
8a7(rgyud): nA~M/puSh+pa pU dza me g+ha sa mu drA s+pha ra Na sa ma ye hU~M/oM na mo nI lA~M pa ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni  maN Da la de ba tA nA~M/d+hU pa ni ra ya ta yA mi hU~M/oM na mo na nI lA~M ba ra d+ha rA badzra pA Ni maN Da la da de ba tA nA~M/oM A lo ke ni ra ya tA yA mi hU~M/oM 
8a8(rgyud): na mo nI lA~M ba ra ng+ha ra badzra pA Ni maN Dal de ba tA nA~M/gan d+ha ni ra ya ta yA mi hUM/oM na mo nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ne maN Da la de ba tA nAM/ni we d+ye ni ra ya ta yA mi hU~M`/oM na mo nI lI ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Nim Na Da la de ba tA n-I/sha ba ta ni ra ya 
8b1(rgyud): ta yA mi hU~M//oM na mo nI lA~M bar d+ha ra badzra pA Ni maN Da la de ba tA nA~M/tsa ri yA nir ya ta yA mi hU~M/oM na mo nI lA~M par d+ha ra badzra pA Ni ma Na Da la de ba tA nAM/ta ri nir ya ta yA mi hU~M`/oM na mo nI lAM bar d+ha ra badzra pA Nim Na Da la de ba tA
8b2(rgyud): nA~M/bA sa na nir ya ta yA mi hUM/oM na mo nI lA pa ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni man Da la de ba tA nA~M/ga na d+ha ri nir ya ta yA mi hu+A~M/oM na mo nI la ba ra d+ha ra badzra pa Ni maN Da la de ba ta nA~M/s+pha ra sha nir ya ta yA mi hU+au/oM na mo ni lA~M bar d+ha ra badzra pa
8b3(rgyud): Ni maN Da la de ba tAnA~M/na na ra ta na sarba bstu nir ya ta yA mi hU+o~M/de'i sngags kyi mchod pa las//sdig pa bshags pa'i sngags kyis su//phyag rgya nges par rab bcas pa//sdig pa bshags pa'i+i smre sngags kyis//lus ngag
8b4(rgyud): yid gsum btud pa yis//yang dag par ni bshags par bya//bsod nams rjes su yid rang dang //yongs su bsngo dang skyabs 'gro dang //sems bskyed pa dang gnas bzhi dang //gong du bstan pa'i phyag rgya nyid//skabs
8b5(rgyud): 'di dag tu shes par bya//dang po rgyu las skyes pa las//phyi nang dngos por mi gnas shing //rang bzhin 'od ni gsal bar bsgom//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las mchod pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////
8b6(rgyud): de nas lag na rdo rje yis//gsang ba'i mgon la 'di skad gsol//bsam pa rab tu rdzogs pa yi//nang gi rnal 'byor gsung du gsol//bde gshegs gsang bas bka' stsal pa//gang zhig bsgrub par bya ba la//thabs
8b7(rgyud): kyi dngos grub 'grub par 'gyur//dang po shes rab can dag gis//bdag gi dngos por snang ba las//de nyid ngang la mchog nyid kyis//'od zer bsil ldan yang dag bsam//de la gang du gyur pa ni//dbugs rgyu 'gugs
8b8(rgyud): par nges par bsam//de las gsal ldan rigs lnga pa//stong pa'i ngang las mngon song pa'i//gzugs bzangs ma sogs rig ma yis//mi 'chi lnga ldan dus 'gyur las//'dzin pa can la sbyin par bsgom//
9a1(rgyud): de nas sngags 'dis rab bsngags te//bdag nyid rigs su ldan pa yis//bdag pos phyag rgya nges par bsam//oM a b+hi Shiny+tsa tsa tu mAM badzra pA Ni hUM/a he a ha 
9a2(rgyud): raM ta tha s+thi to yaM a ho ma ma bri yang tu ti hUM//bdag nyid dngos po la brten nas//mtshan mar mi gnas ma skyes pa'i//gdong nas grub pa bsam bya ste//sangs rgyas dngos la rab sbyor bas//dang por dngos dag bsam 
9a3(rgyud): byas nas//bdag nyid gong gi gzugs gnas la//steng du mig 'dra'i dngos po bsgom//de las gong du gyur pa las//'jigs byed rigs dgu nges par bsam//mig 'dra'i sbu gu rab sbyar bas//snying po'i steng du 
9a4(rgyud): gsal bar snang //snying po'i steng du chu bsgrubs pas//yan lag stong dang ldan pa nyid//spun la phyogs par kha bltas nas//bdag las gong du gyur pa yi//hUM gnyis las bsgrubs steng ma yi//nges par 
9a5(rgyud): gsal byed sbyar ba las//dngos po'i snying por rab song ste//de las mi 'chi 'od ldan pas//'dzem byed steng na rab gnas pa'i//mig 'dra'i yan lag brgyad dang ldan//'joms byed mig 'dra gar mar sbyor//thabs 
9a6(rgyud): dang shes rab gsum du nges//dngos po gsum las rab brgyud de//snying po'i steng na rab gnas pa'i/mig 'dra'i steng du nges par song //rang gi gar ma'i khyon tsam la//skad cig bye brag bcu drug pa'i//
9a7(rgyud): thabs su yongs su rdzogs pa ste//dang po dngos por brtag mi bya//rdzogs pa nyid la me 'bar bas//rim pa de nyid mi gnas shing //dngos po'i snang ba gang yin pa//mar me'i rtse mo lta bu yi//phyi nas rang 
9a8(rgyud): bzhin 'od gsal ba'i//dang po rab tu brtag par bya//rim pa gsum pa rab rdzogs yin//steng du baM gnyis rab 'bar dang //hUM las skyes pa thub pa'i sku//yang dag pa ru brtag bya yin//'og kyang gong ma'i 
9b1(rgyud): sa bon las//bskyed par bya ba kho na yin//sems dpa' gnyis su nges pa dang //'khor lo spros pas char lta bu'i//lo tog shin tu smin byed pa'i//skyon rnams spangs nas de nyid du//slar yang ngo bos yang 
9b2(rgyud): dag gnas//de ltar gsal bar ma 'khrul par//skyon gyis ma gos dri med do//mkha' la kun zhes sems kyis su//rdo rje lam du rab zhugs nas//nor bu dri med zla ba yis//las la sbyor ba sna tshogs pa//
9b3(rgyud): mnyam par sbyar bas bde ba'i mchog/mi 'dzag gnas dang ldan par 'gyur//mkhas pa sangs rgyas bde bas bde//lus la chags par mi lta zhing //sems la chags pa chen por bskyed//de 'dra'i spyod pas 
9b4(rgyud): yongs bsdus nas//gang zhig mya ngan 'das pa las//mchog 'gyur mi gnas mya ngan 'das//sa la mngon par mi gnas shing //kun du'ang mngon par mi gnas pas//ye shes rdo rje go 'phang nyid//brgyud pas 
9b5(rgyud): mngon du byed pa la//'di la rdzas la sogs pa yi//'di yi dngos grub gang yin pas//nges par 'grub pa smos ci dgos//shes rab can gyi las 'di las//chags pa chen por yang dag bsgrub//lus la me 
9b6(rgyud): 'bar chen po yi//snying po 'di nyid bzlas pa las//rang nyid pad ma gsang ba yi//drag po'i rtags su nges pa ste//de bzhin rtag tu bsgrub par bya//sngags bzlas cho ga gsum las ni//khyad par nges par dmigs 
9b7(rgyud): byas la//bdag nyid tha mal gzugs kyis gnas//de dag phan tshun brgyud pa yis//zo chus brgyud pa bzhin du bsam//drag po'i bzlas pa de nyid dang //rang bzhin rdo rje'i bzlas pa yis//rigs gsum yongs 
9b8(rgyud): su bsdus pa yin//oM b+ho b+ho bA nA nA ga a d+hi pa ta ye//i dan nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra sha rI ra/badzra pA Ni hri daM yaM//yaHsa mA ti krA mA sa mA ku la u da kA rAM b+ha bi/She te/tad+ya thA/bud d+ha bud d+ha/bi bud d+ha/ma te krA ma thA/ha ra ha ra nA ga nAM/
10a1(rgyud): @#//pra ha ra pra ha ra nA ga nAM/ts+tshin da ts+tshin da/nA ga hri da yA ni/b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha/nA ga ma rmA Ni bi ShI ri na ya tu nA ga sha rI raM/bi s+pho Ta ya nA ga nA ya nA ni/tad+ya thA hUM phaT/nA ga bi da ra NA ya hUM phaT/nA ga god sa da nA 
10a2(rgyud): ya hUM phaT/nI lAM ba sa nA ya hUM phaT/a pra ti ha ta ba la ba ra kra mA ya hUM phaT/kri tAn tA ya hUM phaT/kri trA na ta ru pA ya hUM phaT/ro Sha NA ya hUM phaT/trA sa nA ya hUM phaT/kuM b+ha Na Da ud sA da nA ya phaT/pre ta bi shI tsa bi nA sha 
10a3(rgyud): ka rA ya hUM phaT/yak+Sha a pa sma bi nA ka ka ra ya phaT/ha na ha na hUM phaT/ha sa ha sa/dA ra dA ra/mA ra ya mA ra ya nA sha ya nA sha ya/sarba big+h+nA bi nA sha ka rA ya phaT/sarba sha trUM saM g+ha bI na sha ka rA ya hUM phaT/i d+ha na nI lAM ba ra d+ha ra badzra pa Ni rA dz+nyA pa ya ti 
10a4(rgyud): swA hA/oM daM Sh+TA ka ra lo ka b+hi Sha Da nI lA ba ra d+ha ra badzra pA Ni ya/nA ga a lAM kA ra sab ta ke tu byA g+h+ra tsa ra ma Ne bA sa nA ya b+ha ma kA ra ga ru Ta ra si ti/kro d+ha mur ti na trai lo kya da ha ya/b+h+ya ma man tra a sa na ru dri ya/nA 
10a5(rgyud): ga tsa tu ra ma b+ha k+Sha ya hUM phaT/oM trai lokya bi dza ya hUM phaT/oM ma hA ka la ra Sha NA ya hUM phaT/oM be sha ra man ye hUM phaT/oM a mri ta kuN Da li ha na ha na hUM phaT/oM badzra yak+Sha hUM phaT/oM badzra ya be ta li ni hUM phaT/
10a6(rgyud): oM badzra DA kA ya hUM phaT/oM badzra b+hai ra ba ya hUM phaT/oM badza pra bya ta ba ra ya hUM phaT/oM ma no hA ra ya hUM phaT/oM sa man ta b+ha tai ya hUM phaT/oM sha ba da kI t+yai ya hUM phaT/oM bran pa t+yai ya hUM phaT/oM shIs t+yai 
10a7(rgyud): ya hUM phaT/oM rA ga ya ta brA ya ni hUM phaT/oM mnas b+ha k+Shi Ni hUM phaT/oM tsaN Da ma hA ro Sha Na sarba sid d+ha ya hUM phaT/e ma Ni d+ha ri badzra Ni hUM phaT/oM bA su ra ta na bi ta hUM phaT/oM a mri ta sid d+hi hUM phaT/oM sam 
10a8(rgyud): sho d+ha ya ba ra ba ra hUM phaT/oM tsa ba sha sa ba ni ra hUM phaT/oM ku mA ra tsa sha ma ni hUM phaT/de nas phyogs bcu 'khrugs pa las//yi ge nges pa gang yin pa//bla ma'i man ngag rab gsal bas//gcig tu 
10b1(rgyud): bsdus pas bzlas par bya//rang 'byung sngags pas bdag nyid la//grangs tshed gzung ba gang yin pa//rigs kyi dbang gis rnam par bshad//kun du snang ba gang yin pa//rigssu bsdus nas ngag gi yang //
10b2(rgyud): spyod yul ma yin tha mal gnyid//drag po'i snying po nyid dang yang //rang bzhin rdo rje bzlas pa yis//rigs gsum yang dag bsdus pa yin//gong ma'i sngags la rab sngags pa//mtha' ru kun la hU~M gis 
10b3(rgyud): brgyan//de mthar bskrad pa'i tshig ngag gis//yongs su brgyan pas nges par bzlas//gang dang gang la las sbyor ba//de dang de las man ngag gis//mngon par shes pas yang dag bshad//bzlas pa phun 
10b4(rgyud): sum tshogs ldan pas//rab 'bring tha ma ldan pa yi//dang po sbyor ba nges pa dang //mtha' mar las la 'jug pa la//grangs dang cha ru rnam par ldan//spyod yul bsdus pa nges gsal ba//bdag gis gang la brtag 
10b5(rgyud): par bya//dang por srid la chags pa dang //bar du gang zhig gnas pa nyid//tha mar 'jig pa gang yin pas//sngags rnams thams cad rgyas par gsungs//'byung ba lnga dang rab ldan nas//de las chos rnams thams 
10b6(rgyud): cad las//yongs su 'byung bas yang dag gnas//de dang rab sbyar dang po'i don//'di las grub par gyur pa gang //las rnams sna tshogs gang yin pa//gdon mi za bar nges pa yin//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can//
10b7(rgyud): rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las nang dang sngags kyi le'u ste lnga pa'o////lag na rdo rje gos sngon gyis//bcom ldan mgon la 'di skad gsol//sems can smin par bya ba'i phyir//'jug pa'i thabs nyid gsung 
10b8(rgyud): du gsol//kun mkhyen mgon pos bka' stsal pa//dang por sa brtag la sogs pa//nges par gong du bstan pa ltar//las rnams nges par rdzogs par bya//sa phyogs dben pa'i khyim rnams su//pa yi rnam lnga la sogs

12a1(rgyud): @#//spangs//oM A kha~M bI ra hU~M//oM ya tha ku la ta tha a hA//sangs rgyas kun gyi phyag brnyan 'bar//de nyid du ni slob ma 'grub//dar la sogs pa sngon po yis//slob pa nyid la dbang bskur la//
12a2(rgyud): sngags 'di yis ni byin brlabs la//dang po gnyis sbyar dar bzung nas//sngags kyi yang dag bskon par bya//mkha' lding sngon po 'dab rgyas pa//mgul ba dar sbyangs ldan pa yis//bu nyid la ni nges par 
12a3(rgyud): sbyin//dngos grub sna tshogs 'grub pa dang //gdon rnams thams cad 'joms pa dang //be con sngon po dbyig dang ldan//me tog sngon po'i phreng bas kyang //de nyid la ni bskon byas nas//sngags dang phyag rgya 'dir ldan 
12a4(rgyud): pa'i//bu nyid la ni sbyin par bya//dang po gsum pa rab sbyar bas//spyi bo'i steng du nges bzung ste//de ni byed pa po la bzhag/bdag nyid yan lag rigs don gyis//dbus kyi g.yon rab grang byas la//rigs tshang yan lag 
12a5(rgyud): bcings pa yis//phyar te byed pa po la bzhag/de yi stod la gnas bya ba//tshangs pa chen po mthong byed dang //mchod pa kun gyi sdud pa yis//dar ram ras sam gang rung ba'i//sngags chus dag byas legs bkab 
12a6(rgyud): nas//bzhi ru nges par gcig pa yi//dngos po gang dag gdags par bya//phyag rgya 'da' min grags par ldan//bya pas sngags bzlas yang dag ldan//kun la khro gnyer brjid par lta//oM u da ra saM g+ha ba b+hu ya hU~M//
12a7(rgyud): oM ga ru Da sarba nA ga b+hak+Sha ya hU~M//oM ba ra~Md na d+ha ka sarba sid d+hi brA pa ta hU~M//oM a dz+nyo ba ya ti lo ka ba shi ka ra ya hU~M//de yi lag pa sgrom byas la//bdag gi drag po'i lis sbyor pa//de yi sgrom nas drangs byas la//
12a8(rgyud): snying po 'di nyid nges par sbyin//oM tsa Na Da ma hA ro Sha Na badzra pA Ni de ha da ha//sarba bag+hi naM ha na ha na sarba//sarba ar+tha shI g+h+raM ku ru ye hU~M//de nas legs pa'i mtshan ma la//chos la dbang sogs mi gnas na//'jug

13a1(rgyud): la thug nas mar la byung //de tshe mi gnas bsam dang bral//'dod chags dag dang ldan gyur na//gcig shos len pa'i yon tan ldan//rtse mor ma dang phaT ldan pas//tsaN+Da la yi drod byung nas//sems 
13a2(rgyud): dpa' ye shes rtse la zung //de yis yang sprul gnas su bkug/snyoms par zhugs shing g.yo bas bzlog/tha ma'i yi ge 'gro 'ong ni//zhu ba'i bsam pas bya bar gsungs//de nas de bsang dngos grub 'gyur//yang na 
13a3(rgyud): 'og gi thig le bzhi//rlung de sprul pa'i 'khor lor 'dus//dro bar byang chub sems bkug nas//rtse la phyin nas nub byang bsam//slar log phar 'gro yang dang yang //tha ma snyoms par zhugs grub 'gyur//yang na gnas 
13a4(rgyud): gzhi gsum po dag/gang yang rung bas steng du phyin//slar log rdo rje rtse la bton//tshur log gnas nas las rnams byas//des ni dngos grub chen po yang //rdo rje bzhin du 'grub par bsam//khams gsum thams 
13a5(rgyud): cad dag pa dang //zhing rnams gtsang mar skye bar 'gyur//bsam gtan de dang ldan pa'i tshe//gzhan pa'i yul du 'grub 'gyur spang //spang ba ma yin blang ba min//snyoms pa de yi ngang la bzhag/gzugs dang sgra 
13a6(rgyud): la sogs pa yi//rang gi sems g.yeng 'gyur ba'i tshe//bstan pa'i rang bzhin med par bsam//bar du so so'i thig le bsam//thams cad sgyu ma lta bur brtag/dang po dang po'i dus dag tu//'dod pa'i yon tan lha 
13a7(rgyud): mor bsam//des ni rtog bzhi bar 'gyur//lag na rdo rje gos sngon po can rdo rje me lce'i rgyud las thams cad rdzogs pa chen po'i rim pa rnal 'byor pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas lag na rdo rje yis//
13a8(rgyud): bcom ldan mgon la 'di skad gsol//'jig rten kun la phan gdags phyir//gtor ma'i rim pa gsung du gsol//kun mkhyen mgon kyis bka' stsal pa//rnal 'byor rnams la phan gdags phyir//bdag gis 
13b1(rgyud): gtor ma bshad par bya//phan gdags pa dang tshar gcad pa//phan pa mchog dang thun mong ngo //gsang ba bla med mchog tu 'dod//phyi nang thun mong shes par bya//rgyas dang bsdus dang shin tu bsdus//tshar gcod pa yang 
13b2(rgyud): shes par bya//dgug dang bskrad dang gsad pa dang //gnan dang bsreg par shes par bya//rgyun dus chen po'i gsum nyid dang //chung ngu rgyun nam dus chen gyi//bcu gnyis bzhi'am tha ma gcig/rtsa ba lnga'am yan 
13b3(rgyud): lag lnga//'bru dang shing tog sman rnams dang //chang 'thun 'bur ba la sogs rdzas//de stengs dkar gsum klu la shis//khrag sna snying sna sha sna gsum//de sogs mthu rdzas tshar gcad pa'i//bskyed rims dang ni ldan pa 
13b4(rgyud): yis//bla nas bsrung ba byas pa las//cho ga gsum du byas pa'am//yang na skad cig lhar bskyed la//mtshan dang ldan pa'i pad ma ru//rdzas rnams kun kyis bkang nas ni//zla ba rdo rje hU~M gis mtshan//mtho gang la ni ri bong 
13b5(rgyud): can//de la bcu drug bdun pas mtshan//rdo rje mtshan ldan pa ru ni//sdud spyod 'phro ba'i mthing dmar dkar//bad 'od ye shes lnga ldan gsum//gru gsum 'og nas zla gam can//thugs kyi mthing nag 'phro ba'i las//
13b6(rgyud): rdo rje lte ba skul mtha' gsum bskyod//dmar po 'bar bas mtshan ldan khrol//thugs kyis bsdus shing gsung gis sbyangs//sku las 'phros pas ri 'od bskul//shes rab can las 'od zer 'phros//de yis yab yum 
13b7(rgyud): bskul nas ni//kha dog dkar po'i bdud rtsi 'bab//dang po skul byed 'od du zhu//khong seng gnas pa pad mar 'bab//bab pa'i gting la de nyid ltung //lan gsum bskor bas 'o ma bzhin//bzhi pa'i dang po gnyis par 
13b8(rgyud): mtshan//de nas gdan bskyed 'khor bcas bgug/dzi baM rdo rje khong sengs kyis//bzhi pa'i dang por mnyes par bsam//yang na yud tsam lhar bsam la//thugs kar nyi ma de steng hUM//'od kyi chos 'byung gru gsum sprul//

33b8(rgyud): hU~M hU~M phaT//kI la ya kI la ya sarba pA ba~M A dz+nyA pa ya ti/kA ya bA ga tsi ta badzra ki la ya hU~M phaT/rdo rje tho bas brdung bya ste//oM badzra mud ga ra badzra kI la ya A ko Ta ya hU~M phaT/spyi bor btab pas nges par rlag/slar 
34a1(rgyud): @#//'dus phyogs mtshams ma lus par //sa gzhir ba dra ba dang //gur bcing ba dang bla re dang //mda' dang me yi 'od 'bar bsgom//oM badzra me di ni badzri b+ha ba badzra b+han d+ha b+han d+ha hUM/oM badzra pra ka ra hUM baM hUM/
34a2(rgyud): oM badzra paM tsa ra hUM laM hUM//oM badzra bi d+han hUM khaM hUM/oM sha raM dzA la traM saM traM/oM badzra dz+wa la a na lA rka hUM hUM hUM rim pas brjod la snying rdza yi//bdag nyid gzhan la phan 'dod pa//smon lam las byung rdzu 'phrul mchog/yaM

34a8(rgyud): chen ldan//dbu skra kham ser gyen du 'bar//nag po sbrul lus klu chen brgyad//sku smad stag gi lpags pas dkris//chos gos sngon pos stod dkris te//mche ba gtsigs shing spyan gsum dmar//'dod 
34b1(rgyud): dgur sgyur zhing brkyang bskum zhabs//mtha' gnyis spangs pa'i steng du bzhugs//de yum rdo rje bde ma khro//dmar ljang zhal gcig phyag gnyis ma//g.yas pa gri gug yab la 'khyud//g.yon pa thod pa bde ba ster 
34b2(rgyud): gnyis med g.yon gyi phyogs nas 'khril//rdo rjes sbyor ba'i sgras bskul te//shar phyogs pad mar nyi zla las//hUM las khams gsum rnam par rgyal//dkar po zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa//g.yas pa rdo rje rtse

35b1(rgyud): ga d+ha ma ni/bi si ma d+ha nA ga sha ri raM/bi s+pho Ta ya nA ga nA ni/tad ya thA/oM A hU~M phaT/nA ga pi na ra NA ya hU~M/nA ga da sa da nA ya hU~M phaT/nI lAM bas nA ya hU~M phaT/a pra ti ha ta bal par kra ma ya hU~M phaT/kri tAn tA ya phaT/

39b3(rgyud): rtog pa rten can bsgom 'dod pas//sku yi gnas su la bor lo ni//dkar po rtsa 'dab sum bcu gnyis//mtshan gyi thabs kyi chos brgyan pa//gsung gi gnas su rtsa 'dab ni//bcu drug dmar po shes rab ste//de 
39b4(rgyud): la thabs kyis nges par brgyan//thugs kyi gnas su dus sbyor brgyad//gtso bo'i yi ge brgyad gnas pa//phrin las bya ba'i gnas dag tu//dbyug gu'i grangs kyi rtsa gnas pa//shes rab gnyis 'gyur kha dog 
39b5(rgyud): dmar//de dbus gnyis med g.yas dang g.yon//thabs dang shes rab rnam par dbye//rtog pa sad pa'i 'khor lo ni//thabs la drug dang shes rab drug/tha ma bde ba 'dzin pa'i yis//mi rtog dpa' bo gnas pa 
39b6(rgyud): yin//yi ge bzhi yis brgyan mdzad de//bde 'dzin 'khor los yi ge med//brjod pa thams cad spangs pa ste//'dus ma byas pa'i rang bzhin mchog/khu ba khrag dang chu ser gnas//'di ni lam gsum 'joms 
39b7(rgyud): byed pa'o//skad cig rlung gi rta yis ni//thabs dang shes rab gnyis bskul nas//gnyis med ye shes skye bar 'gyur//ye shes kyis ni rtog pa sreg/thal ba'ang dmigs par mi 'gyur bas//rtags lnga rim pas 
39b8(rgyud): 'byung bar 'gyur//de nas dpal gyi yon tan te//yang nas yang du goms par bya//sde ba bzhi yi longs spyod ni//gnas su yang dag skye bar 'gyur//gnyis med bde ba'i gnas thob nas//skye 'chi la sogs rgyun chad

41b4(rgyud): zhing //yo dang dma' bar gyur na ni//dngos grub med cing rtsod la gdung //sbom pas nad 'byung phra bas ni//nor nyams gal te chad pas ni//'chi 'gyur g.yas bskor ma bris na//phur bus btab pa yin zhes bya//shar

41b8(rgyud): rgyas chos dang dge 'dun te//skyabs gsum dag kyang bdag la gsol//thar pa chen po'i grong mchog tu//mgon pos bdag ni gzhug par mdzod//ces pa lan gsum bar du bya//pu tshur theg pa chen po yi//

209a3(rgyud): rdo rje rab dgyes brgyan byas pa//rdo rje ston ka dbus su bsam//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//ye shes snang ba rab sgrub pa//yi ge yi ni phyag rgya 'dis//nyid kyi snying khar rgyas btab na//sangs 
209a4(rgyud): rgyas ye shes mchog byed pa//de yis ston ka ma dngos grub//sde dbang lhar bcas lha rnams dang //gnod sbyin srin por bcas pa dang //sbrul dang mi'am ci gnas pa//thams cad 'di yi g.yog tu 
209a5(rgyud): 'gyur//'di ni 'od zer rab tu 'bar ba yis//sangs rgyas mchog gi thugs ldan de 'gro ste//sna tshogs rab 'byung dpal gyis yang dag khyab//chos mchog dam pa byed pa gtong byed cing //spyod bcas mi 
209a6(rgyud): spyod 'jig rten snang byed de//sprin dang bral ba'i zla ba'i 'od mchog byed//mkha' dbyings rnam par bsgyings pas rab dga' bas//A li kA li pad mo bde mchog la//myong zhing mchog byas bskal par 
209a7(rgyud): gnas par 'gyur//'di yis sdig pa 'phrog byed de//'di yis myur du dga' bar 'gyur//'dis ni bde ba'i ngo bo de//'di yis dngos grub grub par 'gyur//skye ba'i sdug bsngal rgyab phyogs pa//tshe 
209a8(rgyud): 'di nyid la 'thob par 'gyur//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje shin tu dgyes pa ye shes kyi snang ba dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya chen mo ste//thams cad mkhyen pa ye shes 'grub par byed pa'o////
209b1(rgyud): dang po oM dang yang dag ldan//tha mar dgyes pa'i yi ge gnyis//rdo rje shin tu dgyes pa che//dbus su gsal ba'i yi ge bsam//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//sna tshogs chen mo'i mdun du 'gro//sna tshogs 
209b2(rgyud): chen mo 'di yis ni//sna tshogs ye shes dngos grub bo//sngags mkhan nyid kyis byin brlabs pa//sna tshogs bdag po chen por 'gyur//sdig pa'i yan lag kun spangs te//bsod nams kun gyi yan lag dge/
209b3(rgyud): kun tu 'gro ba'i dngos grub ni//'di nyid kyis ni rab tu 'grub//sna tshogs sgyu ma'i bde chen gyis//sna tshogs chen po'i bskal par gnas//rdo rje'i bdag nyid brgyan pa yi//bud med kun dang rtsen par 'gyur//de nas de yi 
209b4(rgyud): chos dbyings gnas//dbang phyug mchog gi dngos grub bo//pad mo'i mdun du thog ma'i lha//byas te rdo rje bsgrub par bya//mthe bong srin lag de phyung ste//kha yi nang du gzhag par bya//chad bzhin myang bar byas nas ni//
209b5(rgyud): mod la 'dod pa'i gzugs su 'gyur//A li kA li rab sbyor bas//bud med rnams dang rtsen par byed//blo ldan rdo rje sems dpa' yi//'di ni shin tu dgyes zhes bya//bsil ba dga' byed chen mo ste//sna tshogs 'di ni 
209b6(rgyud): 'gro la gnas//dang po oM ni gnas pa ste//tra ta dang ldan tha mar bya//rdo rje shin tu dgyes che ho//sgeg mo dbus su bskul ba'o//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//bsgegs par bya ba sprul pa yin//'di dag mnyam par 
209b7(rgyud): sbyor ba yis//sgeg mos ye shes bskyed par bya//rdo rje blo ldan brtan po yis//bde bar 'dod pa'i pad mor gzugs//'dod pas 'dod pa spyad par bya//myur du 'dod pas dngos grub bo//de nas rdo rje 'dod dpal te//
209b8(rgyud): bde byed thams cad nye bar sgrub//sems can kun gyis 'dod skyed pa//bde ba chen por de 'gyur ro//mthu yis pha rol phyin pa'i mchog/pad mo'i tshul du bsgegs pa yis//rdo rje sbyor thabs la gnas pas//rdo rje 
210a1(rgyud): chen po de dngos grub//ci dgar 'dod pa'i 'dod chags kyis//rtag pa'i lam dang yang dag ldan//rtag pa'i chos kyis rjes 'thun spyod//bsgrubs na rtag pa rtogs par 'gyur//'di yis kun tu bzang 
210a2(rgyud): zhes bya//de ni dngos po kun yin te//'dod pa med cing nyon mongs med//nga rgyal med cing 'dzin med pa//sangs rgyas mchog tu rtag pa yi//sangs rgyas chen po de yi dngos//dpal ldan thog ma tha ma med//chos 
210a3(rgyud): rnams kun gyi rjes 'thun pa'i//'dis ni dngos po thams cad kyi//sangs rgyas bdag por bdag nyid 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad dang //'di la khyad par cung zad med//shin tu dgyes pa chen po rdo rje bdag 
210a4(rgyud): po'i dpal shin tu dgyes pa sgeg mo dpe med pa dang ldan pa'i yi ge'i mchog gi sku'o////dang po oM bcas lha mo yi//tha mar tsheg dreg mi phyed gnyis//de gnyis dbus kyi phyogs nyid du//he zhes bya ba rnam 
210a5(rgyud): brgyan te//shin tu dgyes pa'i rdo rje che//bzhad pa nyid ni rab tu sbyar//yi ge yi ni phyag rgya 'dis//nyid kyi snying la rgyas btab na//phreng ba'i rgyan gyis rnam brgyan par//dpal ldan de ma thag tu 'gyur//yid kyis 
210a6(rgyud): ji ltar bsams pa bzhin//de ni sangs rgyas yul du 'gro//de ni de phyir sangs rgyas mthong //chu skyes de dang rtsen par byed//de nas 'dus pa dgyes 'gyur te//sangs rgyas yang dag zhing nyid nas//sangs rgyas rnams 
210a7(rgyud): kyis bde ba che//dngos grub rnal 'byor de la sbyin//byed pa dang ni zlog pa dang //bde ba chen po 'phrog pa yis//rnal 'byor gsang ba'i dbang phyug la//gsang ba chen pos mgu bar byed//thabs bcas shes rab 
210a8(rgyud): chen po la//rgyal ba nyid kyis stobs byed de//A li kA li mnyam sbyor bas//bde ba chen po mgu bar byed//bde ba thabs kyi dngos grub rnams//sgyu ma'i tshul gyis bsgrub par bya//yi ge yi ni phyag rgya 'di//
210b1(rgyud): shin tu dngos grub mchog tu brtag/rdo rje shin tu dgyes pa'i bde ba'i phreng ba rgyas pa dpe med pa dang ldan ma zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya ste/las thams cad kyi don gyi dngos grub sbyin pa'o////oM ni dang por 
210b2(rgyud): gtsug tor bya//te ta skad snyan tha mar te//dbus su he dga' zhes bya ba//bde chen dga' ba rdo rje dbyings//rdo rje shin tu dgyes che yi//phyag rgya 'di ni rab dgyes 'brel//bris pa dang ni 'dzin pa dang //
210b3(rgyud): dran pa yis kyang dngos grub ster//sdig pa kun las grol byed cing //bsod nams thams cad nye bar 'gro//mi dge thams cad ma lus spong byed cing //sems ni phyag rgya'i yi ge brgyan te 'gro//'jig 
210b4(rgyud): rten thams cad kun tu bkur sti byed//rig ma stong gis mngon par mchod byed cing //rig 'dzin mu sman bud med rnams kyis ni//g.yag rnga dkar po'i tshogs kyis g.yob byed pa'i//'jig rten dbang po bde 
210b5(rgyud): mchog 'brel ba 'thob//blo ldan nam mkha' ngos ni thams cad du//rdo rje 'dzin gyi dgyes pa'i rnal 'byor ma//yid du 'ong ba'i spyan ni rgyas pa dang //rnal 'byor spyod pa'i bde bas 'bigs pa yis//mgul 
210b6(rgyud): nas 'khyud pa'i rtsen pas rol bar byed//pi bang gling bu ya mtshan sga snyan gyis//glu blangs dngos grub rgya che thob par byed//ji snyed yid du 'ong ba'i glu dag ni//'jig rten grags pa'i bde ba'i bde ba dag/
210b7(rgyud): bde ba'i sgra yis bskyed par rnams bsgyur ba'i//de dang 'thun pa'i dbyangs 'dis sgrub par byed//Sha Ta pa da gar dza'i dbyangs//glu ru blangs pas pad mo gnas//dngos grub myur du 'thob par byed//'di 
210b8(rgyud): yis byin rlabs dag pa dang //'di yis byin rlabs bde ba'i rnams//sbyor ba 'di yis sbyar ba yis//'dod byed thams cad 'thob par 'gyur//shin tu dgyes pa chen po rdo rje sems dpa'i mchog tu dgyes 
211a1(rgyud): @#//pa chen mo'i dbyangs dpe med pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i yi ge'i phyag rgya'o//de bzhin oM ni dang por te//dbus su he dgyes bde ba che//rdo rje gar byed de pa gnyis//'di ni thams cad bde byed de//
211a2(rgyud): sangs rgyas rnams kyi rab dgyes nyid//rnal 'byor yi ni rgyas btab na//bde dang ldan par byed cing 'gro//'og min gyi ni gnas chen du//sangs rgyas rnams dang thabs gcig 'grogs//bde byed chen po'i dbang 
211a3(rgyud): chen dang //chos kyi rgyal po bde chen 'thob//ji snyed gar gyi gtsug lag las//sgyur bar byed pa bshad pa dang //ji snyed de snyed phyag rgya'i rnams//gnyis min 'dus pas 'dis dngos grub//kye ma'o dgyis pa 
211a4(rgyud): bde byed che//kye ma'o bde ba bde chen po//kye ma'o 'dod pa 'dod chags che//kye ma'o sna tshogs bdag nyid che//de la sogs pa tshad med pa//'jig rten gyi ni yo byad rnams//pad mo gsal ba gar gyis 
211a5(rgyud): ni//rnal 'byor gar byas dngos grub po//sangs rgyas kun gyi phyag rgya 'di//sna tshogs gar ni ston byed pas//myur du gar ni mang byas pas//gar gyi gtsug lag dngos grub po//rdo rje shin tu dgyes pa 
211a6(rgyud): chen po'i dpal bde ba chen po dga' byed gar zhes bya ba'i yi ge phyag rgya phan tshun bcu brgyal mtshan dang ba dan gsum pa'o//bcu gsum yi ge dang po nyid//rdo rje shin tu dgyes ba che/
211a7(rgyud): rdo rje'i sku ni sna tshogs che//tha mar 'bar dang 'dra ba'i dza//nam mkhar zla skar 'dra ba yi//phyag rgya'i ye shes rmad 'byung ba'i//'di yis thabs kyis mnyam bzhag ste//gzung ba gsal bar gzung ba yi//tsha ba'i 
211a8(rgyud): gser gyi gzi brjid 'dzin//ye shes snying khar bsam par bya//nam mkha' nyi ma bum 'dra bar//de ni de ma thag tu 'gyur//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad nyid//shin tu rmad 'byung thob par 'gyur//
211b1(rgyud): rnal 'byor kun gyi bde 'brel ba//rdo rje blo mchog dngos grub po//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin dge yi//sna tshogs tshon po thob 'gyur nas//'dod pa'i chos kyis mi gos blo//'dod pa chen po sten par byed//de 
211b2(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po shin tu dgyes pa chen po dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i rdo rje ma lus pa lus pa med pa'i rdo rje'i sku dpe med pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i yi ge chen po'i phyag rgya'o////
211b3(rgyud): de bzhin oM ni gzhag bya ste//rdo rje shin tu dgyes pa che//rdo rje'i sgra ni bde 'brel ba//tha mar khro bcas sbyar bar bya//phyag rgya 'di ni bde mchog ste//bar bcas pa yang thos par 'gyur//khams gsum 
211b4(rgyud): ngo bo thams cad ni//sgra yi thos pa dngos grub po//'di yis sangs rgyas thams cad nyid//tshe 'di nyid la dngos grub ste//bud med pad mo ud pal dang //ma lus rgyal ba'i rnams dang rtsen//rdo rje pad 
211b5(rgyud): mo'i sbyar bas na//dga' byed myur du dngos grub po//rab tu dgyes pa chen po dpa' rdo rje sems dpa'i tshad med pa'i yon tan gyi sgra dpe med pa dang ldan pa shes bya ba'i don gyi dpal thams cad grub par byed 
211b6(rgyud): ma'o//oM ni spyi gtsug thig ler babs//rab dgyes chen mo rdo rje can//rdo rje dri bder 'bel ba yi//tha mar gnas sher rab sbyar bya//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//sna tshogs chen mo mdun du 'gro//
211b7(rgyud): sna tshogs chen mo 'di yis ci//sna tshogs ye shes grub par 'gyur//bdag nyid rdo rje brgyan pa yi//de ni mtshan ldan kun dang rtsen//de nas chos dbyings gnas pa des//A li kA li mnyam sbyor bas//
211b8(rgyud): dga' byed lhan cig rtsen byas na//dbang phyug dam pa dngos grub 'gyur//'di ni rdo rje sems dpa' yi//blo ldan rab tu dgyes zhes bya//rdo rje'i dri dpe med pa de//ldan ma zhes bya ba'i yi ge'i phyag rgya'o//
212a1(rgyud): @#//o mchog gtsug tor rnams byas te//shin tu dgyes che dbus nyid du//rdo rje ro ni sbyor che ba//tha mar ho ste thig le gnyis//'di yis rab tu dgyes par 'gyur//dpal ldan dpe med 
212a2(rgyud): bde ba dang//bcom ldan rdo rje sems dpa' nyid//sna tshogs sbyor ba sna tshogs bdag//sangs rgyas kun gyi rol pa yis//mchod pa mi zad byed par 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnams kyi gnas thams cad//
212a3(rgyud): nam mkha' mnyam pa nyid thob bo//rab tu dgyes pa'i dbang po shin tu dgyes pa chen po'i ro mchog gi rgyal mtshan dang ldan pa zhes bya ba sems can thams cad kyi don thams cad grub pa'o//de bzhin 
212a4(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i bdag po zla gsang thig le bshad pa'i rgyal po las//chos kyi 'khor lo ste gnyis pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das shin tu dgyes pa 
212a5(rgyud): chen po dpal rdo rje sems dpa' kun tu bzang po nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thus rdo rje las shin tu dgyes pa chen pa gdon mi za ba'i rgyal po zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po bshad nas cang mi gsung 
212a6(rgyud): bar gyur to//de nas nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad kyi rdul phran dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad gsang ba'i gzungs dang g.yog tu bcas pa rnams kyis yongs su bskor ba/
212a7(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor chen po dang bcas pa rnams kyis mdun du byas pa ma lus pa lus pa med pa'i nam mkha' la 'gro ba dang//nam mkha' la 'gro ba'i tshogs dang ldan pa//phyi nang gi 
212a8(rgyud): pho nya dang pho nya mo rab tu rgyas pa rnams shin tu ngo mtshar rmad du gyur te//dgyes shing spyan ni rgyas pa yis//bzhengs nas dgyes pa chen po yi//brjid pa'i phyag gis gar mdzad cing//thal mo 
212b1(rgyud): sbyar nas gtsug nyid du//bzhag pa yis ni 'dun pa byed//bskul ba yis ni dbyangs 'di yis//de dag gtso la mnyes par byed//kye ma'o chos ni ngo mtshar che//kye ma'o spyod pa ngo mtshar che//kye ma'o
212b2(rgyud): sbas pa ngo mtshar che//kye ma'i bde ba yang dag 'byung//kye ma'o kun tu bzang po'i don//kye ma'o rab dgyes yang dag 'grogs//kye ma'o bde ba dag pa che//kye ma'o bde ba bde mchog ste//kye ma'o sngags kyi
212b3(rgyud): yang dag sbyor//kye ma'o snying po yang dag bsdus//kye ma'o phyag rgya mnyam par bzhugs//kye ma'o sgrub pa rmad 'byung ba'o//kye ma'o pha rol phyin ma yin//kye ma'o ji ltar mthu rig ma'i//kye ma'o sna
212b4(rgyud): tshogs dgos pa che//kye ma'o dag pa gsum sbyar ba//kye ma'o rdo rje mnyam sbyor ba'i//kye ma'o pad ma rnam phye bas//kye ma'o dbang po gnyis sbyor ba'o//kye ma'o mchog dang mnyam pa'o//kye ma'o
212b5(rgyud): gar ni dag pa che//kye ma'o glu rab dri ma med//kye ma'o sil snyan rnam par 'brel//kye ma'o brdab dang mun pa sel//kye ma'o dag byed me tog phreng//kye ma'o pog snod dge bste//kye ma'o mar me
212b6(rgyud): mun pa sel//kye ma'o dri ni shin tu bsil//kye ma'o rnal 'byor mnyam par sbyor//kye ma'o sgyur ba nam mkha' 'dra//kye ma'o sangs rgyas rdo rje mchog/kye ma'o rin chen bde mchog skyed//kye ma'o sna tshog+sa
212b7(rgyud): pad mchog ldan//kye ma'o sna tshogs sgyu ma mdzad//kye ma'o ngo mtshar sangs rgyas dang//kye ma'o ngo mtshar dpa' po rnams//kye ma'o ngo mtshar sems dpa' rnams//kye ma'o ngo mtsha ra lha yi rnams//shin
212b8(rgyud): tu dgyes che rdo rje la//rgyan pa kun gyis bstod byas te//rdo rje sems dpa'i 'gying ba yis//dga' dang bde byed skyes par 'gyur//de nas pha rol phyin thams cad//'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dgyes 'gyur te/

213b7(rgyud): chos kyi phyir ni gsol ba 'debs//dkyil 'khor dam tshig bdag nyid la//bcom ldan dgyes pas bshad du gsol//gang gi sems can bde nyid kyi//bde bas dkyil 'khor bsgrub par bgyid//de nas

214b3(rgyud): btab de bzhin rmongs pa dang //gso bar byed dang za ba dang //phan tshun du ni skyed par byed//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje chen po'i dbang bskur ba rdo rje'i phreng ba zhes bya ba shin tu dgyes pa bde ba thob

215a2(rgyud): rgyas kun gyis dbang bskur bas//dbang phyug dam par de 'gyur te//thams cad dag pa mos pa yis//pad mo dmar po mchog 'dzin 'gyur//pad mo sgyu ma rnam mdzad pas//sna tshogs chen por de 'gyur ro//sdug
